9a1(1): @#//gteg mod kyi//'khor ba'i 'khor lor bskal pa gcig las 'dzin mi nus//khyod kyis stobs bcu'i gser ni skyon med rgyal ba'i gdung //shin tu lcug cing brtan la 
9a2(1): dge ba'i dpal dang bcas//bag chags mtshams sbyor spangs shing tshogs gnyis mthu ldan te//grangs med bskal par 'gro ba smin mdzad sgrim mi 'tshal//rang gi stobs rdzogs dge ba'i blo gros sa 
9a3(1): la bzhugs//gzhan gyi blo zhan brdzun dgu shes pa'i stobs kyang bskyed//rang gi grol la gzhan yang rab tu sgrol mdzad pa//de la 'khor gsum dang zhing gus par phyag kyang 'tshal//chu ni rang bzhin 
9a4(1): gsal la gzhan rnams skyed byed de//skam spangs kyis 'dzin chen dga' bskyabs na sa yang g.yos//'jig rten phyogs gteg 'gro ba'i gnas su gyur mod kyi//'khor ba'i 'kho lor bskal (pa)gcig
9a5(1): las 'dzin mi nus//khyodakyi smon lam yongs su dag pa'i tshogs kyi chu//yon tan lo tog skyed cing 'bras bu chen po bsgrub//skyed rgu tha ba khengs pa'i rang bzhin byams kyi brlan//ngan 'gro'i   
9a6(1): rnam par 'gyes pa bsdu ba'i rnam bzhis bsdus//smon lam chu chen brlabs kyis sems can mthar skyod na//snod gyur phyogs kyis dad pas byang chub mchog tu g.yo//thog ma sems bskyed dus 
9a7(1): nas seng ge'i sgrar bsgrags pa//bskal pa grangs med mtha' ru rtogs med ngang gis bsgrub//rang gi smon laM rdzogs shing mi g.yo sa la bzhugs//skye bo gzhan yang yi bsad gnyid myos snon du bcud
9a8(1): rang yang grol la gzhan yang rab tu grol mdzad pa//de la 'khor gsum dang bas gus par phyag kyang 'tshal//sa ni dngos po thams cad 'grub ba'i 'byung gnas te//rtsa ba gzung dgu rang   
9b1(1): gi bskal pa smin cing rgyas//skye dang 'jig pa kun kyang de la snang mod kyi//'khor ba'i 'khor lor bskal pa gcig las rgyun mi gnas//khyod kyis thabs mkhas sa las yon tan mchog kun
9b2(1): 'byung //dge rtsa grangs med brtan zhing rgyas par de la gnas//'dul ba'i dbang du btang dang bsgrub pa sna tshogs ston//bskal pa grangs med mthar spyad thabs kyi thar mi thug/rang gi thab+sa 
9b3(1): mkhas rdzogs shing ring song sa la gnas//gzhan yang blun chos khong sgril 'byin cing 'dor du bcud//rang yang grol la gzhan yang rab tu grol mdzad pa//de la 'khor gsum dang bas gus
9b4(1): par phyag kyang 'tshal//lcags ri dam pa khor yug lhun stug 'phan med pa//chu chen yongs su 'dul ba rlon zhing dang bar byed//rkyen las med dang mtshon gyi rgyur yang 'gyur mod kyi//rang bzhin 
9b5(1): lhun la yon tan bskal pa gcig tu zad//dmigs pa kun spangs khyod kyi shes rab lcags kyi ri//zhi gnas bsam gyis mi khyab 'dzin cing rnam dag ste//sgrib pa gnyis po ma lus   
9b6(1): gcod cing snang byed pa//dbang bsgyur yon tan tshogs dang dus kyi mtha' mi bas//rang gi shes par rdzogs shing mngon pa'i sa la gnas//gzhan yangagati mug bsgrib pa'i btsan zhan spyod du   
9b7(1): btsud//rang yang grol la gzhon yang rab tu grol mdzad ba//de la 'khor gsum dngabs gus par phyag kyang 'tshal//rgya mtsho zabs dang ldan pa che ba'i brlung gis grags//de ni rin chen sna
9b8(1): tshogs kun gyis 'byung gnas yin zhes brjod//'gro la ngo mtshar gnas shing gzugs brnyan snang mod kyi//'khor ba'i 'khor lor bskal pa gcig las gnas mi nus//khyod kyi bsam
12a1(1): @#//'bel /snying po yangs pa dpe legs mdog 'jam ze ba ring //'dam gyi nyes bas de la dri mar gos pa med//me tog che ba bzang rgyas gam yo'i lhum las 'phags 
12a2(1): lta su bkra shis 'byed 'dzums dus dang mthun ba ste//phal las 'phags pa'i chu bskyed rin chen pad+mo'i mchog/ma rgad shel sdong dam pa me tog pad+ma ris//ze ba gzer bzang de'i snying po an+da  
12a3(1): snyil /mthong na dpal skyes rgyal sras kun gyi gdan du 'gyur//de ni dpa' mo nges min khyod kyi yon tan tshogs//dri med dam chags thugs dang bsod nams mtha' yas brten//dge rtsa yongs 
12a4(1): su dag pa chu ba dam pa yin//byang chub spyod pa rnam rdzogs 'dab ma rgyas pa ste//ze ba gtams dkrigs nyams pa med pa'i 'khor gyis tshogs//snying po de ni byang chub snying po rdo rje'i gdan /
12a5(1): 'gro ba yid 'ong byams pa chem pa' mgon po khyod//'jig rten gsum na bzhugs kyang 'jig rten chos kyi 'gro//lha dang mis mchod btsun pa rgyal thabs dam pa mnga'//de yang mi 'pham

12a7(1): yongs kyang sel//de 'dra yon tan tshogs chen mnga' la phyag kyang 'tshal//spyod pa rin chen sna tshogs yon tan mthu ldan pa//'phrul gyis rnam par sprul cing gdan gyi pad+mor gda'  /
12a8(1): dge ba sna tshogs mthus grub dpe med yid du 'ong //dpal ldan gnan chen de ni bde gshegs sras kyi rkyen//spu sdug g.yas g.yon rin chen ldum bu dag/mthong dga' reg 'dod    
12b1(1): gzhon sdug rnam pa bkra zhing rgyas//rgyu nyid rin chen tshon bzang sdug gu 'phel ba'i tshogs//gnyen 'jam mi ri khu ba ro bzhim dag dang ldan//de dag ldam ldum nges min byang chub yan   
12b2(1): lag rnams//chos rab rin chen me tog sdug gu bzang bar sbrul//gyi nom lha sras yon tan chog ldan rgyan yin te//rgyal sras yon tan chen po mda' ba'i rkyen du byung //de na phye 
12b3(1): leb sbu sdug dbyibs legs lnga zhing 'khor//spyod lam mdzes dgu ston cing me tog ngad zhim 'jib//bung ba stag ris 'phur zhing sgra snyan bsgrogs pa dag/ldum tog chang du   
12b4(1): bskyabs shing sbrang rtsi'i tshogs kyang 'jibs//nges par srin bu'i rigs mi gnod sbyin klu la sogs//gang la bskal pa yod dgur 'khor zhing bsnyen bskur bgyid//khyod 
12b5(1): las chos kyi me tog bdud rtsi'i khung mnos shing //bdag dang gzhan gyis ched la tshogs gnyis brtson par brtsogs//de dag rgyal ba'i byin brlabs byin tu brlabs pa na//la la 
12b6(1): rdzu 'phrul spyod lam mdzes kyis 'dul bar byed//bar bar sems can don phyir chos ston sgra yang 'byin//kun kyang mtha' ru bla med sa mchog thob par 'gyur////yo byad ldem 
12b7(1): pa'i le'u ste bzhi pa'o////khyod kyi sku ni shin tu dpe legs mdog kyang gsal//gzi brjid che mnga' yid du 'ong blta ngoms mi myong //
12b8(1): sha rus gzugs 'dra pha ma'i rgyus la byung ba min//thabs mkhas dam pa yab yin shes rab chen po yum//pha rol phyin mchog byams pa'i rigs las byung bas na//cis   
13a1(1): @#//'dul sna tshogs mchog gi sku ston bsam mi khyab//bshos bu 'bru la sogs pa'i zas la gsol ma lags//smon lam rnam par dag pas yongs su bsgrub 
13a2(1): pa'i sku /skyes bu dam pa'i mtshan mchog rdzogs par mnga' ba la//dpe byad bzang po brgyad cus mdzes par brgyan pas na//'khor los bsgyur ba'i lus la 'chang ba'i mtshan   
13a3(1): rnams las//zhing du gsal zhing legs par bsgrub par 'phags par dga'//dka' thub brtul zhugs bskal ba bsam yas dpag 'das su//'grol phan phyir khyod kyis yongs su 
13a4(1): su mdzad pas na//mtshan ldan zhabs gnyis shin tu brtan par gnas pa ste//'jam zhing sdug pa pad+ma'i mdog ltar yid du 'grub//'khor ba'i 'jigs pas gnyen pa'i 'gro ba bskyabs 
13a5(1): tshol rnams//mi 'jigs skyabs kyis bskyabs shes mgon mdzad bsrungs pas na//dga' ba 'khyim dang 'khor lo dam pa mtshan kyi rnams//zhabs kyi mthil la dpe legs shin tu 
13a6(1): mdzes par grub//spyod pa dam pa'i bsdus pas dus rnams thams cad du//khyod kyis 'gro ba phongs pa dga' dang bde ba'i don//rdzogs par mdzad pas yid 'od legs par grub 
13a7(1): pa yis /phyag dang zhabs sor dra bas 'brel ba mchog tu gyur//thugs rje chen po mnga' bas phyag ring pus mo 'das//byams pa rgyas pa mnga' bas sku mdog gser ka 'dra//yo byed 
13a8(1): thams cad btang bas rnam pa bdun du mtho//sti stang mgon mdzad ro bstod legs pa seng ge 'dra//dbyen spangs 'dum pa gsungs pas tshems sdug bzhi bcu tshang //bde gsheg+sa 
13b1(1): brten bzang bstan pas spyan gsal dkar nag 'dres//bcom ldan gsungs gsal brjod pas ljags ni che zhing yangs//dpe byad mtshan rdzogs sku mchog mnga' la phyag kyang 'tshal//
13b2(1): I'khor los bsgyur ba'i rgyal po gling bzhi'i dbang byed pa//dge bcu las lam sngon tshe gus par spyad 
13b3(1): pa'i mthus rin chen bdun la spyod pa'i 'jig rten mthu mchog thob//nye ba'i rin chen bdun po de dag rkyen du grub//gser gyis 'khor lo rin chen gling bzhi dbang byed dang //glang po 
13b4(1): cang shes thal kar yon tan mang ldan gnyis//rta mchog rin chen dkar po bgrod dgur mgyogs pa dang //bud med rin chen yid 'ong yon tan 'grangs dang bzhi//nor bu rin chen zur 
13b5(1): brgyad 'od mchog ldan pa dang //khyim bdag rin chen dgos dgur 'byon par 'bul bas drug/blon po rin chen ma sgo yid bzhin bsgrub par bdun//rin chen de dag gzi ldan longs 
13b6(1): spyod rkyen du grub//nye ba'i rin chen ral gri rang 'gro byin can dang //gnyen pos mi tshugs lpags pa gta' che mchog dang gnyis//nye ba'i rin chen mal mchog yid 'ong dam pa dang 
13b7(1): 'dod pa lnga ldan nags tshal mdangs dga'i gnas dang gzhi//nye ba'i rin chen khyim rab rmad chos ldan pa dang //gos mchog thags su ma btags bkra shis yang dang drug/nye ba'i rin   
13b8(1): cen lham mchog dpag tshad brgya 'gro bdun//yo byad 'kho ba de dag nye bar longs spyod rkyen//'phags pa mi 'pham dga' ldan chos kyi 'khor lo rje/bdud bzhi btul zhing kun la(sa) 
14a1(1): @#//rgyal ba 'gro ba'i gtso//nor gyi 'khor la rjes ltar nyon mongs ldan ma lags//'byung ba gsum na rgyal por brjod dgu dper mi bzod//rin cen mchog
14a2(1): mnga' bdun las 'khor lo rin cen phul//byang chub 'tshang rgyar gzhol ba'i dge chos 'khor lo ste//nam mkha' mtha' gtugs phyogs rnams yongs la dbang sgyur bas//gling bzhi dbang 
14a3(1): byed gser gyi 'khor lo dper mi bzod//rin chen glang por mnga' ba mnyam med theg pa gcig/gti mug mdongs pas rnams na thad dgur rtog pa'i rgyu//theg pa de la rigs bzhi skyes bu brtan par
14a4(1): bzhog/glang po cang shes thal kar dam pa dper mi bzod//rin chan rta por so sor yang dag rig pa mnga'//'gro ba skal yod thogs med gnas su skyel//dgrod dka' chos lam bgrod la
14a5(1): mthu che mgyogs pa ste//rta mchog dkar po mgyogs de dper mi bzod//rin can btsun mo'i mchog tu khyod la dpal mnga' ste//yid du 'ong dgu'i bde ba ma lus 'phral du bsgrub//
14a6(1): sems can kun byang chub dam pa'i bde ba stobs//bud med rin chen yon tan bgrangs pa dpe mi bzod//rin chen nor bu dam pa sna tshogs 'od mnga' ste//skad cig yud kyis 'jig
14a7(1): rten rgya mtsho kun tu snang //de la reg pas 'gro ba sems kyi snang ba 'thob//rin chen zur brgyad yon tan mang yang dper mi brjod//rin chen khyim bdag tu ni chos kyi spyan mnga' ste//
14a8(1): de bzhin gshegs kyi snying po gter na 'chang ba gzigs//lnga phur dri mas yogs pa ma lus yongs su spyod//dpag tshad khor yug gter mthong dper mi bzod//rin chen blon por
14b1(1): khyod la byin brlabs (mchog)mnga' ste//ci dang ci bzhed de dang de dag mnyes par mthul//rgya che yangs pas bsam pa'i spyod yul kun las 'das//phyogs cig nyi che'i don bsgrub blon po dper mi    
14b2(1): bzod࿒ /rin chen ral grir 'jig rten 'das kyis shes rab mnga'//spyod pa kun gyi gnas su de nyid rang dbang 'gro//sna tshogs (tshogs)chos kyi sde kun 'gal med ro gcig 'gyur//gzhan gyi rin 
14b3(1): chen ral gri shas kyi dper mi bzod//rin chen lpags pa tri sna 'dzina'i g.yang gzhi bsnams//gta' chen sgang smug lo ba dkar (ru)ring phyogs phyogs 'chus//me dang chu yis 'jig ce 'am rnyigs 
14b4(1): shes dengs mi srid//phul nas yun rings bzod mchis gser gyis tshul du bda'//drang srong brtul zhugs mtshan mar rtag (tu)bsnams gyur pa//lpags pa'i yon tan mang la chags shing  
14b5(1): srid ma lags//spyod ldan dad dang ri dgas dge bsam rdzogs slad du//smon lam bsod nams 'bras bu che bsgrub gzhan don mdzad//g.yas ru g.yas su 'chungs pa dge bsrung mchod 
14b6(1): kyi dper//g.yon ru g.yon du mchus pa mi dge spangs kyi tshul//rgyabaM sgang smuganag nyon mongs rgyab tu bor ba'i mtshan//lto dgar gsal ba rnam byang mdun du blangs kyi rtags//ring   
14b7(1): lon ma rnyengs byang chub sems bskyed mi bzang tshul//mgo mjug tshang ba dgos pa thams cad rdzogs pa'i dpe//byol song cig gis phyir yang lpags pa rtag snams na//skye bo gzhan 
14b8(1): gyi don la brtson pa smon ci 'tshal//khyod ni dge tshogs tshad med sa'i dam pa ste//mgon po bsod nams zhing la phul ba'i dge ba ni//lpags pa'i spu dang rdul rnyed ri dgas de la 'phel  
17a1(1): @#/ O /chos kyi 'khor lo bar chad skabs med rgyun du 'khor//chos dbyings spor med bum pa zlum po'i tshul du snang//yang dag gnyis su med pas yid bzhin

17a8(1): shing dogs pa'ang skyes//du rim spel ba bsrings na spa zhing mdzes par snang //bsnyen na ltar yod tsam las zhe ste gzung du med//shes rab ldan pas mthong na rang bzhin nges par rtogs//
17b1(1): byis pa glen pa rnams ni de la rmongs shing zhen//khyod kyi thugs rje 'jig rten snod gyur ldan pa na //'dus byas chos kyis rol cing sna tshogs ston mdzad pas//de dag yon tan mchog snyam dga'

17b3(1): sgyu ma bzhin//ye shes mchog gi spyan gyis chos kun 'ja' 'drar gzigs//byis pa mi shes rmongs kyang the tshom btsal cing bcad//thogs med mkhyen bsal de 'dra phul mnga' ba//'ja' dang nam 
17b4(1): mkha'i tshul las dper bla bstod mnyam na//chos kun 'ja' 'drar ston pas sems can bsgrol gyis lhag/'dra ba'i dper blang dpe zla med pas khyod la ston//'ja' dang mkha'i (dpe'i)le'u ste//

17b8(1): tshogs dbang po'i rjes su mthun mdzad cing //dus gsum chos mkhyen dbyings kyis ye shes dag bskyed pa//skye bo rin chen rgya mtshor bsgyur la phyag kyang 'tshal//yon tan sprin gyi le'u ste brgyad pa'o////
18a1(1): @#//kha ba khu 'phang se ba dus dus 'bab pa ni//ri ka'i sa rdo rtse mtho rang rong mnyam snyoms pa//g.ya' brag g.yag 'dren nag po gangs kyi tshogs su byas//chu'i  
18a2(1): klung bskyed tha ba dgon gdung yul du 'gyur//de bzhin mthu chen bskyed med kha dang ser 'bab ste//nga rgyal snyems pas mtho bas rtse mo rnal du mnyam//nag po bdud kyi phyogs kyang byang chub   
18a3(1): gangs su bskyed//rigs pa'i chu bskyed 'gro ba bsod nams zhing du 'gyur//mthu stobs chad med che ba'i kha ba ser bab pa//sngo zhing rgyal gyis thub med mad kyi mthus 'dul zhing //'gro ba

18a7(1): de dag dper brjod byang chub phyogs su 'dra ba ste//gzhan gyi spyod dang mi mnyam khyad zhugs rnam pa mang //ri bo dus 'dzin mdangs 'bar nye bar gnas pa ni//ngos bzhi dri med   
18a8(1): gsal bar phyogs rnams kun tu snang //lhun sdug mdzes pa'i yon tan brjod pa gang yang grags//phyi nang gdags sribs par gyi sgang mtho legs dang ldan//dran pa nye gzhag 
18b1(1): rnam par byang ba'i tshul brtags na//lus 'di mi gtsang dngos kyang chos sku gtsang ma'i rten//dngos nyid mi rtag lags kyang dkon mchog gdungs sob bstab//kun rdzob don dam rnam 
18b2(1): pa du mar thugs su chud//chos dran nye bar gzhag bde sdug bsngal gnyis min la//'dod chags zhe sdang gti mug rang bzhin rnam pa mkhyen//nam na ci tshor sems can thugs rje gzung 
18b3(1): bzhin tshor//bdag dang gzhan gyi nyon mongs sna tshogs yongasu spang //sems kyi dran pa nyer gzhag yang dag tshul brtan pas//'gro ba kun gyi phyi nang dmigs sems yongs su mkhyen 
18b4(1): thugs rje chen po tshul bzhin kun la so sor skye//'on kyang de la mi gnas bye brag 'byed mi mnga'//chos kyi dran pa nye bar gzhag pas dngos po rnams//stong nyid chos kyi dbyings dang mi 
18b5(1): gnyis thugs chud pas//rnam pa thams cad ma lus mkhyen pas rtog pa med//sems can kun don mdzad kyang chos nyid mtshams mi mnga'//sngon gyi smon lam mthu ldan dran pa nyer 
18b6(1): gzhag bzhis//log pa rnam bzhi ngan lta kun ni yongs su sbyangs//rnam rtog mtshan mar 'dzin pa ma lus btsal cing bor//spyod pa rgya chen sna tshogs chub par yongs su dag/khyod 
18b7(1): kyi yon tan tshogs chen mnga' ba de dag la//mthun pa'i tshul las dran te dper blang ston snyam na//mi mnyam mnyam pa dpe las 'das pas brjod du med//'on kyang nam gyis brtag+sa 
18b8(1): pa'i dper blang 'ol tsam bstod//gshol 'dzin ri bo tshul las ming brtags pa//skyon med dbyibs legs mdzes pa mthong dgus bsngags par grags//bye brag rnam pa phyogs 
19a1(1): @#//bzhi kun nas rgyas par bres skyel dang //'bying gi gnod pa kun spong tshul du gnas//yang dag spong ba ming dang yon tan mthun pa ste//mi dge go bkag dge chos spel 
19a2(1): bas kun tu bzung //tshul khrims ting 'dzin shes rab rnam grol yongs su rdzogs//rnal 'byor dam pa rtag tu 'khrul med sgrib mi 'tshal//mi dge 'byung ba ma skyes mi 'gag tshul mkhyen 
19a3(1): pa 'dod chags khong 'khro gti mug dri ma yongs su bsal///dus gsum ma lus mnyam pa nyid du mngon sum phyir//'das dang ma 'ongs da ltar byung la ye shes 'jug/dge chos ma skyes bskyed pI 
19a4(1): don du thugs bskyed pas//yang dag bsgrub pa'i dge ca skyon med brtan par btsugs//byang chub gsum du 'gro ba ma lus dgod slad du//dge chos mang po bskyed ces rnam par dgongs pa mdzad 
19a5(1): dge chos skyes pa mi nyams rgya cher spel skyes pas//khams gsum spyod yul kun las 'das pa'i bsod nams tshogs//bdag dang gzhan don rgya che grangs med 'grub pa'i rgyu//byang chub
